			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesThreeACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

				}					   
			}

		which = cosmoSAWivesFourACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesFourACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFourACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFiveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoSACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesEightACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesEightACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesEightACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

			value = 280
			}
			}

		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}

						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16b

					}
				}				
			}																
				}

		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesOneCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_fortification_expert
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesElevenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesElevenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

		}

			check_variable = {

			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesTwelveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesTwelveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

	}

		}
		}

		name = cosmo_satrader_events.1500.a

			limit = {

					any_province = {

		}
		}

			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15a
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.1510
			}
		}
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_satrader_events.1500.b
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15b
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15b
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.1520
			}
		}
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_satrader_events.1500.c
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15c
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15c
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.1530
			}
		}
		}		
		option = {
		name = cosmo_satrader_events.1500.7
			if = {
				limit = {					
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16c
					}
				}
				}

			check_variable = {

			}

		}

		}

			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFifteenCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}

			}
			}

		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}
